UKR_fedrada_category = {
	UKR_subsidies = {
		icon = GFX_decision_ukraine_two
		days_remove = 220
		cost = 50
		ai_will_do = { base = 300 }

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_authority_more_60
				check_variable = { ROOT.subjectavtoritet > 59 }
			}
		}

		allowed = { NOT = { original_tag = UKR } }
		visible = {
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					has_autonomy_state = autonomy_republic_ukr
					has_autonomy_state = autonomy_novorossiya_ukr
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_subsidies"
			UKR = { country_event = { id = subject_ukr.4 days = 1 } }
		}
	}

	UKR_buildings = {
		icon = GFX_decision_ukraine_two

				tooltip = UKR_authority_more_60
				check_variable = { ROOT.subjectavtoritet > 59 }
			}

		visible = {
			ROOT = {
				OR = {

	# Promote Russian Interests
	UKR_promote_russian_interests = {
		icon = GFX_decision_recogn_russia_button
		fire_only_once = no
		cost = 50

		visible = {
			country_exists = SOV
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = SOV
			}
		}

		days_remove = 30
		days_re_enable = 10

		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = UKR_dissolved_verkhovna
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_presidential_decisions_only_one_is_available_TT
				NOT = {
					has_decision = UKR_promote_western_interests
				}
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_promote_russian_interests"
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.03
			}
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = UKR }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^0 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^1 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^2 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^3 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^4 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^5 = USA }
						}
						UKR = {
							check_variable = { influence_array^6 = USA }
						}
					}
				}
				newline = yes
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -2 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = UKR }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = { base = 1 }
	}

		allowed = { always = yes }

			check_variable = { treasury > 0.01 }
		}

				date < 2016.01.01
			}
		}
		available = {

				date > 2015.01.01
				date < 2016.01.01
			}
			controls_state = 1089 #Poltava
			has_army_experience > 4
		}
		modifier = {
			research_speed_factor = 0.02
			industrial_capacity_factory = -0.002
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				state = 1089
			}
    		highlight_color_while_active = 3
    		highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		days_remove = 60
		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_modernize_brdm_2vepr"
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			1089 = { #Poltava
				add_state_modifier = {
					modifier = {
						local_resources = -0.01
						local_factories = -0.02
					}
				}
			}	
		}
		remove_effect = {
			army_experience = -2
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "BRDM-2 Vepr"
				type = medium_tank_destroyer_chassis_0
				parent_version = 2
				modules = {
					main_armament_slot = afv_machine_gun_2
					turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
					chassis_type_slot = tank_wheeled_suspension_medium
					armor_type_slot = tank_steel_armor_gen1
					engine_type_slot = afv_gasoline_engine_gen1
					reload_type_slot = automatic_loading
					special_type_slot_1 = empty #supposed to be afv_reverse_doors but forbids light tank
					special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_2
					ammunition_load_slot = machine_gun_ammo_1
					secondary_armament_slot = afv_coax_machine_gun_2
				}
				upgrades = {
					tank_nsb_armor_upgrade = 1
				}
				icon = "gfx/interface/technologies/UKR/LAND/BRDM-2.dds"
			}
			1089 = { #Poltava
				add_state_modifier = {
					modifier = {
						local_factories = 0.03

					}
				}
			}
		}
